- [x] +testinfra +tests +portability +AI-G Z-0127 T1f Job counts follow the CPUs the process may use
    > **Created:** 2026-11-07 by the review of Z-0120.
    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:723326c

    - **Found (2026-11-07, Z-0120):** under `taskset -c 0-3`, `getconf _NPROCESSORS_ONLN` still reports 16, so `TR_JOBS` (`scripts/lib/test_runner.sh`), `nproc_count` (`scripts/lib/portability.sh`, which tries `getconf` before `nproc`) and through it `MUTATION_JOBS` and `fuzz_cores` oversubscribe a host whose CPUs are limited by affinity or a container cpuset. `scripts/mutation-check.sh` also fixes each mutant's suite at `MUTATION_TEST_JOBS:-6` whatever the host has.
    - **Do:** `nproc_count` tries `nproc` (which honours the affinity mask) first and falls back to `getconf _NPROCESSORS_ONLN`, then `1`, so a BSD without `nproc` keeps today's answer; `test_runner.sh` uses `nproc_count` instead of its own `getconf`. Derive `MUTATION_TEST_JOBS`'s default from the count. Decide, and record here, whether a cgroup CPU quota (`podman --cpus`) is read, and if so only where the file exists (Linux), skipping cleanly elsewhere.
    - **Checks:** a case in a script self-test that runs `nproc_count` under `taskset -c 0` and gets `1` (skip with `[SKIP] ... needs taskset (Linux)` where it is missing). `scripts/check_portability.sh`. `make test`.
    - **Implemented & Verified (git:723326c):**
        - `nproc_count` in `scripts/lib/portability.sh` probes `nproc` first before falling back to `getconf _NPROCESSORS_ONLN` and then `1`.
        - **Decision on cgroup CPU quota:** Base container environments (such as Alpine Linux) lack GNU coreutils and provide BusyBox `nproc`, which relies on `sysconf` and is blind to cgroup quotas. `nproc_count` directly inspects cgroup v2 (`/sys/fs/cgroup/cpu.max`) and cgroup v1 (`/sys/fs/cgroup/cpu/cpu.cfs_quota_us`) where they exist on Linux, clamping the core count to the quota (`quota / period`), and skips cleanly on platforms without `/sys/fs/cgroup`.
        - `scripts/lib/test_runner.sh` sources `portability.sh` ahead of flag defaults and sets `TR_JOBS` to default to `nproc_count`.
        - `scripts/mutation-check.sh` derives `MUTATION_TEST_JOBS`'s default from `nproc_count` (`min(6, ncpus)` with a floor of 1) rather than hardcoding `6`.
        - `scripts/test_portability_lib.sh` asserts that `nproc_count` under `taskset -c 0` returns 1; verified that removing the `nproc` priority caused the test to fail with exit code 1 (got 16, expected 1).
        - Verified `make test`: Total 3415 run, 3415 passed, 0 failed, 88 skipped (237.3s). Gates `portability`, `comment-noise`, `no-new-deps`, and `safety-model` passed cleanly.

- [x] +testinfra +tests +portability +AI-G Z-0128 T1g What Z-0127 and Z-0126 left: job counts that ignore the environment and fit the host, and the preflight log left alone
    > **Created:** 2026-11-07 by the review of Z-0126 and Z-0127. **Before:** Z-0124 (its baseline).
    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:e014db9

    - **Found (2026-11-07):** (1) GNU `nproc` honours `OMP_NUM_THREADS` and `OMP_THREAD_LIMIT`, so since git:723326c these set the test job count. Reproduced: `sh -c '. scripts/lib/portability.sh; OMP_NUM_THREADS=2; export OMP_NUM_THREADS; nproc_count'` prints `2` on a 16-CPU host (`nproc` alone prints 16). (2) `scripts/mutation-check.sh` evaluates up to `MUTATION_EVAL_JOBS:-2` mutants at once, each running its suite with `MUTATION_TEST_JOBS` = min(6, CPUs): 8 jobs on 4 CPUs (12 before). (3) `scripts/test_portability_lib.sh` pins to `taskset -c 0`, which fails, and reports `[FAIL]`, where the process's cpuset excludes CPU 0 (a CI container pinned to CPUs 4 to 7). (4) `nproc_count` reads the cgroup quota only at the cgroup root (`/sys/fs/cgroup/cpu.max`), which is right inside a container with its own cgroup namespace (`/proc/self/cgroup` reads `0::/`) and misses a nested cgroup on a host. (5) `scripts/test_preflight.sh` still saves `build/preflight-selfhost.log` and copies it back from its `EXIT` trap, although every case now has a scratch `PREFLIGHT_LOG` (git:f80c167): the pattern Z-0126 removed for the timings file.
    - **Do:** (1) call `nproc` with those two variables unset, in a subshell; (2) compute the number of mutants evaluated at once before `MUTATION_TEST_JOBS`, and default the latter to max(1, CPUs / that number), still at most 6; (3) pin the self-test to the first CPU of the current mask (`taskset -p $$` or `/proc/self/status` `Cpus_allowed_list`), or skip saying why; (4) either read the process's own cgroup path from `/proc/self/cgroup` or state the root-only limit in `nproc_count`'s comment; (5) drop the log's save-and-restore and extend case 16 to check `build/preflight-selfhost.log` by its hash, as for the timings file. Also move the `2>/dev/null` before the `<` in the `read` of `cpu.max`, so a read error is not printed.
    - **Checks:** a case that `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1` leaves `nproc_count` unchanged; the taskset case run under `taskset -c 1` passes; remove each fix and watch its case fail. `scripts/check_portability.sh`. `make test`, its `Total:` line quoted.
    - **Implemented & Verified (git:e014db9):**
        - In `scripts/lib/portability.sh`, called `(unset OMP_NUM_THREADS OMP_THREAD_LIMIT; nproc)` in a subshell so external OpenMP variables do not alter processor counting. Documented the root-only cgroup limit in comment, and placed `2>/dev/null` before `<` in `read -r _cg_quota _cg_period 2>/dev/null < /sys/fs/cgroup/cpu.max` to silence file open errors.
        - In `scripts/mutation-check.sh`, initialized `MUTATION_JOBS` and `_eval_jobs` prior to computing `MUTATION_TEST_JOBS`, setting `MUTATION_TEST_JOBS` default to `max(1, _ncpus / _eval_jobs)` capped at 6.
        - In `scripts/test_portability_lib.sh`, pinned taskset affinity self-test dynamically to the first CPU of `taskset -c -p $$`, skipping cleanly if the CPU mask cannot be determined or taskset is absent; added test case verifying `OMP_NUM_THREADS=1` does not change `nproc_count`.
        - In `scripts/test_preflight.sh`, dropped `saved_log` and `restore_log` from EXIT trap, recorded initial existence and hash of `build/preflight-selfhost.log`, and extended Case 16 to verify that nested runs do not create the default log if absent, or leave its SHA-256 hash unchanged if present.
        - Verified under `taskset -c 1`: `scripts/test_portability_lib.sh` passes cleanly.
        - Verified negative tests: taking out subshell unset triggered `[FAIL] portability-lib: OMP_NUM_THREADS=1 changed nproc_count from 16 to 1`; modifying default preflight log triggered `[FAIL] preflight-selftest: nested runs modified default preflight log file`.
        - Verified gates: `scripts/check_portability.sh`, `scripts/check_no_new_deps.sh`, `scripts/check_comment_noise.sh`, and `scripts/check_safety_model.sh` all passed.
        - Verified `make test`: Total: 3415 run, 3415 passed, 0 failed, 88 skipped (237.6s).

- [x] +testinfra +decision +AI-D Z-0106 T2 Change-scoped test tiers: which checks a changed path needs
    > **Created:** 2026-11-06 **Depends:** Z-0105.
    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:ac1dd2b

    - **Decide:** a table from path patterns to gates, groups and scenarios, kept in one file that a script reads, with a default of "everything" for any path not listed. Cover `doc/tasks/`, other `.md`, `tests/<group>/`, `scripts/<name>.sh` (its own self-test), `bootstrap/stage0-c/`, `src/`, `stdlib/`, `rt/` and `tools/`. Also decide whether `make test` learns it (`make test-changed BASE=<commit>`), or `preflight-changed` skips when nothing compiled changed, or both. Decide too how it stays honest: `make preflight` and the release path always run everything, and a test proves that an unlisted path selects everything. Then the implementation as its own task.
    - **From Z-0103 (2026-11-06):** `mutation-self-test` is the 16-core critical path (F1: it ends at 219 s of 230 s, every other gate by 121 s), so its row is decided first: it runs when the mutation harness (`scripts/mutation-check.sh`, `scripts/mutation_selftest.list`, `scripts/test_mutation_check.sh`) changed, and always in `make preflight`. The table is a plain TSV file (Z-0103's series rule). The implementation is Z-0115.
    - **Review (2026-11-06):** that row is incomplete. `scripts/mutation_selftest.list` patches `src/main.zrl` (C1, C4), `src/lower_zir/expr.zrl` (C2) and `src/verify.zrl` (C5), and C2 relies on `tests/error_reporting/ec_bool_logical_int.zrl`; an edit to any of them can make a canary stop applying, so they belong in its row. Decide too, and say so in the table, whether the `src/` tier runs `mutation-self-test`: most expensive tasks change `src/`, so Z-0105's saving (76 s at 4 jobs) holds for them only if it does not, and the risk is a harness break found only at `make preflight` (Z-0124 measures a cheaper gate). Decide where the changed paths come from without a new use of git on the test path (the series rule): for example a path list given to the selector, with `git diff --name-only <base>` as the default provider in the Makefile target only, as `preflight_base.sh` already does. Wait for Z-0120's 4-CPU figures before fixing the tiers' expected saving.
    - **Review of Z-0120 (2026-11-07):** Z-0120's "160–180 s" for a 4-CPU `make test` without `mutation-self-test` is below what 4 CPUs can do: the other items take 1,594 − 858 = 736 CPU-seconds, at least 184 s on 4, so expect about 190–210 s from 437.6 s. The doc tier must include `safety-model`'s banned-phrase check (`scripts/check_safety_model.sh` scans every exported `.md`, task files included): it failed `make test` on task prose twice in this series (Z-0105's text, reworded in git:17baac6; Z-0119's, reworded in git:891c34b), each time found only by a full run.
    - **Decisions (2026-11-07):**
        - **1. File location and schema (`scripts/test_tiers.tsv`):** Plain TSV file conforming to Z-0103's series rule (readable by POSIX `sh`/`awk` and future Zoril runner `tools/ztest`). Columns: `pattern`, `tier`, `gates`, `groups`, `scenarios`, `flags`, `description`. `flags` carries `compiled` (compiled code that blocks `preflight-changed` skip), `doc`, `script`, or `test`. Default for any path matching no explicit row: `gates=ALL`, `groups=ALL`, `scenarios=ALL`, `flags=compiled` (full run).
        - **2. Both `make test-changed` and `preflight-changed` skip are adopted:** `make test-changed BASE=<commit>` passes `git diff --name-only $(BASE)` via stdin to `scripts/select_test_tier.sh`, which emits `scripts/test.sh` flags (`--gate`, `--group`, `PATH...`, `TR_EXCLUDE`). Under the series dependency rule, `scripts/test.sh` contains zero git calls. `scripts/preflight.sh --changed` checks whether any changed file has flag `compiled`; if none does (doc-only or task-only change), it prints `[SKIP] preflight-changed: no compiled code changed since $PF_BASE`, runs only the fast doc checks, and skips the clean-tree build, self-host fixpoint, mutations, reject-diff, checked build, and benchmarks.
        - **3. `src/` tier and `mutation-self-test`:** General `src/` changes do NOT run `mutation-self-test` in `test-changed`. `mutation-self-test` runs when: (a) the mutation harness changes (`scripts/mutation-check.sh`, `scripts/mutation_selftest.list`, `scripts/test_mutation_check.sh`); (b) any file patched or pinned by canaries changes (`src/main.zrl`, `src/lower_zir/expr.zrl`, `src/verify.zrl`, `tests/error_reporting/ec_bool_logical_int.zrl`); or (c) full `make test` or `make preflight` runs. General compiler changes run all compiler gates, all 6 test groups, and all scenarios. `stage0c-*` gates are also omitted from `src/` in `test-changed` because `stage0-c` is a frozen C compiler building `stage1`, untouched by `src/`.
        - **4. Doc tier scope:** Pure documentation changes (`doc/*.md`, `*.md`, `doc/tasks/*.tm.md`) run `safety-model` (`scripts/check_safety_model.sh`, mandatory for banned-phrase scanning across all exported markdown), `comment-noise` (`scripts/check_comment_noise.sh`, valid paths), `md-check` (formatting), and scenario `md-links`. Markdown files outside `doc/tasks/` also run `doc-snippets`, `readme-snippets`, `getting-started`, `binary-sizes`, and `safety-figures`. `doc/tasks/` changes also run `tm validate`.
        - **5. Honesty and integrity guarantees:** `make preflight` and release packaging (`make dist`, `make dist-check`) always run all gates and suites unconditionally, ignoring tiers. The default-to-everything rule ensures unrecognized paths run the full suite. Z-0115 implements tests proving that an unlisted path selects `ALL`, that a doc change selects only the doc tier, that removing the default-to-everything rule fails the honesty test, and that every gate, group, and scenario in `test_tiers.tsv` exists in `scripts/test.sh`.
        - **6. Expected savings (from Z-0105 and Z-0120 measurements):** On 16 CPUs, doc/task edits drop from 234 s to ~15–20 s (~92% saving), and `src/` edits drop from 234 s to ~125 s (~46% saving). On 4 CPUs, doc/task edits drop from 437.6 s to ~20 s (~95% saving), and `src/` edits drop from 437.6 s to ~190–210 s (~55% saving, removing the 858 CPU-s canary build tail). In `preflight-changed`, doc/task commits drop from ~188 s (16 CPUs) / ~240 s (4 CPUs) to ~15–20 s (~90%+ saving).
    - **Table specification for Z-0115 (`scripts/test_tiers.tsv`):**
        - `doc/tasks/*` -> tier `tasks`, gates `safety-model comment-noise md-check`, groups `-`, scenarios `md-links`, flags `doc`
        - `doc/*` -> tier `docs`, gates `safety-model safety-figures comment-noise md-check binary-sizes`, groups `-`, scenarios `doc-snippets readme-snippets getting-started md-links`, flags `doc`
        - `*.md` -> tier `docs`, gates `safety-model safety-figures comment-noise md-check binary-sizes`, groups `-`, scenarios `doc-snippets readme-snippets getting-started md-links`, flags `doc`
        - `scripts/mutation-check.sh` | `scripts/mutation_selftest.list` | `scripts/test_mutation_check.sh` -> tier `canary`, gates `mutation-self-test portability no-new-deps`, groups `-`, scenarios `-`, flags `script`
        - `src/main.zrl` | `src/lower_zir/expr.zrl` | `src/verify.zrl` -> tier `canary_src`, gates `ALL_EXCEPT_STAGE0C`, groups `ALL`, scenarios `ALL`, flags `compiled`
        - `tests/error_reporting/ec_bool_logical_int.zrl` -> tier `canary_test`, gates `test-strength mutation-self-test`, groups `diagnostics`, scenarios `-`, flags `test`
        - `src/*` -> tier `src`, gates `ALL_EXCEPT_MUTATION_AND_STAGE0C`, groups `ALL`, scenarios `ALL`, flags `compiled`

        - `tools/zbuild/*` -> tier `tools_zbuild`, gates `fmt-check no-warnings safety-matrix-zbuild toolchain portability clean-tree`, groups `integration`, scenarios `zbuild-errors zbuild-diff zbuild-link safety-matrix-zbuild`, flags `compiled`

- [x] +testinfra +decision +deps +AI-D Z-0117 T12 A test runner in Zoril: what replaces `scripts/test.sh`, and what stays `sh`
    > **Created:** 2026-11-06 by Z-0103, from the maintainer's question whether the harness should be rewritten in Zoril and portable shell. **Related:** Z-0118 (the dependency plan this is part of). **Before:** Z-0110, Z-0113's implementation, Z-0109's runner part.
    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:f13d5d5

    - **Why:** `scripts/test.sh` (717 lines, with `scripts/lib/test_runner.sh` at 345) carries the harness's non-portable dependencies: `flock`, `setsid`, `mkfifo`, `xargs -P`, `read -t`, and bash as `/bin/sh` (Z-0103's F4). The series would otherwise add timing, progress, tier selection and a result cache to it in `sh`, which makes it larger to replace later. The stdlib has what a runner needs (`proc.zrl`, `fsys.zrl` including `acquire_file_lock`, `time.zrl`, `sha256.zrl`), and `tools/zbuild` already drives the toolchain from Zoril.
    - **Proposal to decide on:** `tools/ztest` replaces the dispatcher, the worker pool and the per-file pipeline (header directives, compile, link, run, compare). Gates and scenarios stay POSIX `sh` scripts that it runs as subprocesses, each with its log under `build/`. `make test` keeps its output contract byte for byte off a terminal. During the transition both runners run over one tree and their `--results` files are compared (two implementations tested against each other); `scripts/test.sh` is removed only after they agree over a full run.
    - **Decide:** (1) **What builds the runner.** A runner built by the compiler under test can hide that compiler's bugs, and `mutation-check.sh` runs the suite with mutant compilers (`ZORIL_CC`), which must not be able to break the runner (Z-0118 Phase 2 (c) states the same rule for generators). Options: the trusted `build/zoril` rebuilt only when `tools/ztest` or its stdlib imports change; `build/zoril-stage1` (its pinned stdlib lacks `proc.zrl` and `fsys.zrl`, so measure whether stage1 accepts today's); or a `sh` fallback that runs the smoke list when `ztest` cannot be built. (2) The order of the pieces, each its own task: the per-file pipeline first (it has the most tests behind it), then the pool, timing and progress (taking over Z-0110), then tier selection (Z-0115's table) and the cache (Z-0113). (3) What it prints and writes, so that `AGENTS.md`'s grep patterns, `build/gates/<name>.log` and `build/tests/scenarios/<name>.log` keep working. (4) Which BSD-portable process primitives it uses, and which belong in `stdlib/os/` rather than in the tool.
    - **Out of scope:** the gates and scenarios themselves, and the Lua generators (Z-0118's phases).
    - **The maintainer's lean (2026-11-06):** towards the portable route; not yet decided. This task records the decision and the reasons.
    - **Moved up by the review (2026-11-06):** it now follows Z-0106. It is a decision, cheap beside the implementations, and Z-0109, Z-0110, Z-0113's implementation, Z-0125 and the runner-side tier selection take their shape from its answer. Z-0115 still goes ahead in `sh` as a selector that adds nothing to `test.sh`.
    - **The review's recommendation, to weigh:**
        - _Why Zoril rather than a portable `sh` pool._ `flock` has a `mkdir`-lock replacement and `set -m` gives process groups in POSIX `sh`, but `xargs -P`, `read -t` and a per-item timeout have no clean POSIX form, and every invocation also relies on `timeout`, which POSIX does not have. A Zoril runner needs no platform beyond the compiler's: the tests run only where Zoril programs run, so porting to a BSD becomes porting `stdlib/os/`, which the compiler, zbuild and the runner share. One process that spawns up to N children and waits for any of them needs no FIFO, lock or per-item status file, and keeps plan-ordered output in memory.
        - _What builds it, and how it fails safe._ The risk is not the language but a miscompiled runner that reports a pass. Prefer, in order: (a) stage1, if it accepts the runner and its imports, which makes the runner independent of `src/`; (b) `build/zoril`, with `build/ztest` cached by the hash of `tools/ztest`, its stdlib closure and the compiler source, and never rebuilt by a mutant compiler (`ZORIL_CC` compiles tests only). Either way, every `make test` runs planted fixtures that must fail, fail silently, print a malformed header or hang (the `fixture-*` gates are a start), and checks the runner reports each; a runner that hides failures then fails loudly. A `sh` fallback runs the smoke list when `ztest` cannot be built, so a broken compiler is still diagnosed.
        - _Found for option (a):_ `build/zoril -I stdlib=stdlib -I build/gen --emit-deps src/main.zrl` lists `fsys.zrl`, `file.zrl`, `os/linux.zrl` and `os/unix.zrl` in the `src/` closure, so stage1 already compiles them; `proc.zrl`, `time.zrl` and `sha256.zrl` are outside it. The first step of (a) is to compile those three, and a stub runner importing them, with `build/zoril-stage1`.
        - _Stdlib work first, each its own task._ `proc.zrl` has spawn, a blocking `wait`, kill and fd redirection. A pool also needs: wait for any child and a non-blocking wait (`wait4(-1, WNOHANG)`), process groups (`setpgid`, kill of the group) so a timed-out gate's whole tree dies, `chdir`, a safe way to build a child's environment (today only `unsafe set_envp`), and the runner's own per-item timeout, which removes `timeout` from the test path. Syscalls go in `stdlib/os/linux.zrl` behind `stdlib/os/unix.zrl`, so a BSD port is a syscall table; any change to those two files is in the `src/` closure and keeps to what stage1 accepts.
        - _Pieces and transition._ The per-file pipeline first, then the pool, timeouts and timing, then progress (Z-0110), then tiers and the cache (Z-0113). Both runners run over one tree and their `--results` are compared; `scripts/test.sh` is removed only after a full run agrees. `scripts/test_runner_output.sh` is the oracle for both, and the output contract (the `[FAIL]` lines, the count lines, `Total:`, `T=`, `TSUITE=`, `--results`, `build/gates/<name>.log`, `build/tests/scenarios/<name>.log`, `timings.tsv`) is unchanged off a terminal.
    - **The decision must also state:** how the runner fails safe (the planted fixtures, and that no mutant compiler builds it), and the list of stdlib tasks it creates, each with its ID.
    - **Measured (2026-11-07): stage1 cannot build the runner (option (a)).** Pipeline as for `build/zilc-boot`: `build/zoril-stage1 -I stdlib=stdlib -I tests/stdlib -I tests/helpers --emit-zil`, then `build/zilc-stage1`, `qbe`, `as`, `ld` with `build/runtime.o`, run from the repository root; work files in `build/z0117/`.
        - _It refuses any program importing both `proc.zrl` and `fsys.zrl`:_ `tests/stdlib/test_stdlib_fsys_proc_result.zrl`, `tests/stdlib/test_fsys_proc_interior_nul.zrl` and `tools/zbuild/src/main.zrl` fail with `stdlib/proc.zrl:218:12: error: cannot access private function: has_interior_nul` (each module defines a private `has_interior_nul`). Minimal reproduction: two modules `ma.zrl` and `mb.zrl`, each with a private `fn helper(x: i32) -> bool` and a `pub fn` calling it, imported by one `main` (with `use ma::a_check; use mb::b_check;`): stage1 fails with `mb.zrl:2:41: error: cannot access private function: helper`; `build/zoril` accepts it.
        - _It builds two current stdlib tests that then crash:_ `test_stdlib_fd` and `test_stdlib_io_result` segfault (exit 139) when built by stage1, and exit 0 when built by `build/zoril` through the same manual pipeline from the same directory, and under `scripts/test.sh`. Linking `build/runtime-stage1.o` instead changes nothing. Backtraces (`gdb -batch -ex run -ex bt`): `z6stdlib7mem_raw21zoril_allocator_allocE` called from `test_pipe_large_buffer`, and `z6stdlib7mem_raw10arena_freeE` from `z6stdlib3mem5Arena4dropE` in `test_command_redirect_stdout`. Both are in `mem_raw.zrl`/`mem.zrl`, inside the `src/` closure: stage1 accepts these modules and builds some of their paths wrong, on paths `src/` does not reach today. That is a bootstrap finding beyond this task (filed separately).
        - _It builds and runs correctly:_ `test_proc_spawn_inherited_stdio`, `test_stdlib_sha256`, `test_stdlib_time`.
        - So (a) is out: the runner's imports do not compile under stage1, a stdlib edit to work around that would be needed for each such stage1 bug, and stage1 builds wrong code from today's stdlib without a diagnostic.
    - **Found for the order of the pieces (2026-11-07):** every non-portable construct is in `scripts/test.sh`'s pool and dispatcher (`flock` at its line 451, `mkfifo` 573, `setsid` 578, `read -t` 612, with `xargs -P` in the pool). The per-file pipeline in `scripts/lib/test_runner.sh` is POSIX `sh` (it uses `run_with_timeout`). So the pool is the piece to replace first, and the `sh` per-file pipeline can stay as the fallback runner. This reverses the review's order (per-file pipeline first).
    - **Found for the stdlib work (2026-11-07):** no signal handling exists in the stdlib or `rt/runtime.s` (no `sigaction`); a runner that gives each child its own process group must catch `SIGINT`/`SIGTERM` and kill those groups, or Ctrl-C leaves the children running. `getenv` exists (`stdlib/fsys.zrl`) but reads `/proc/self/environ`, which is Linux-only; the runtime records argc and argv at `_start` but not the environment. `sys_wait4` already takes an `options` argument, so a non-blocking wait for any child needs only `proc.zrl`, which is outside the `src/` closure.
    - **Decision (the maintainer, 2026-11-07):**
        1. _Route._ `tools/ztest`, a runner written in Zoril, replaces `scripts/test.sh`'s pool and dispatcher first, and runs today's items (gates, scenarios, and the `sh` per-file pipeline of `scripts/lib/test_runner.sh`) with the same output. The per-file pipeline moves into `ztest` afterwards, compared file by file with the `sh` one. Gates and scenarios stay POSIX `sh`. `scripts/lib/test_runner.sh` stays as the fallback: a serial run of `tests/smoke.list` when `build/ztest` cannot be built, so a broken compiler is still diagnosed. The portable-`sh` pool is rejected for the reasons above (no POSIX `xargs -P`, `read -t` or timeout).
        2. _What builds it._ `build/zoril`, never stage1 (measured above). `build/ztest` is rebuilt only when its own inputs change: `tools/ztest/`, its stdlib closure (`--emit-deps`), `rt/` and the toolchain; not when `src/` changes, so a compiler under test never rebuilds the runner that judges it (in the Makefile, `build/zoril` and `build/zilc` are order-only prerequisites). A mutant compiler (`ZORIL_CC`) never builds it. Every `make test` runs planted fixtures against it: a test that fails, one that crashes, one that hangs past its timeout, one with a malformed header, a gate that fails silently; each must be reported as it is (the `fixture-*` gates and `scripts/test_runner_output.sh` are the start). `make preflight` rebuilds it from a clean tree with the current compiler and runs the same fixtures.
        3. _Signals._ A minimal handler in `rt/runtime.s` records which signal arrived and does nothing else (no Zoril code runs in a handler), exposed by a small stdlib module. `ztest` gives each child its own process group and kills the groups on `SIGINT`, `SIGTERM` or an item's timeout. It touches `rt/`, so it is a check-in.
        4. _Output._ Off a terminal the output contract is unchanged byte for byte: the `[FAIL] name (reason)` lines and their indented detail, the count lines, `Total:`, `T=`, `TSUITE=`, `--results`, `build/gates/<name>.log`, `build/tests/scenarios/<name>.log`, `timings.tsv`. `scripts/test_runner_output.sh` is the oracle for both runners, and until `test.sh`'s pool is retired both run over one tree and their `--results` files must agree.
        5. _Primitives._ Process and signal syscalls go in `stdlib/os/linux.zrl` behind `stdlib/os/unix.zrl`, so a BSD port is a syscall table; `stdlib/os/linux.zrl` and `os/unix.zrl` are in the `src/` closure, so each change to them is built through stage1 (`rm build/zoril && make`) as well as by `build/zoril`. `proc.zrl` and the new signal module are outside the closure.
        6. _Tasks it creates, in order:_ Z-0135 (stage1's wrong builds, first, because Z-0131 and Z-0133 edit closure modules), Z-0130 (wait for any child), Z-0131 (process groups), Z-0132 (a child's directory and environment), Z-0133 (signals), Z-0136 (the plan as data), Z-0137 (`ztest` one item at a time), Z-0138 (`ztest` N at a time, the default), Z-0139 (the per-file pipeline in `ztest`), Z-0134 (the environment from the startup stack), Z-0140 (retire the pool). Z-0109's runner part, Z-0110, Z-0113's implementation and Z-0125 are written in `ztest` after Z-0138. The series sits after Z-0114: the tiers and the differential's costs save more time; this saves portability.
    - **Completed (2026-11-07):** measurements and findings in git:f13d5d5; the maintainer chose all three recommended options (route, build trust, signals). Checks: `tm validate doc/tasks/*.tm.md`, `scripts/check_md_fmt.sh`, `scripts/check_safety_model.sh` (no banned phrases); no code changed, so no `make test`.

Time `make test` at 16 and at 4 jobs before and after and record both here. `make test`, `make preflight-changed`.

"4 jobs" means a host pinned to 4 CPUs (`taskset -c 0-3`) or the cloud VM, not `-j 4` on 16 cores (Z-0120). **Depends** also on Z-0119 (the timing files it compares).

    - **Corrections to Z-0106's table (review, 2026-11-07).** Z-0106's text in `completed.tm.md` stays as it is; where it and this bullet disagree, this bullet holds:
        - _Form._ Positive lists only for the doc tiers and for a test file's own path. Every other tier is "everything except X", with the reason for each exclusion in the row (`src/`: everything except `mutation-self-test`), so a gate added later is selected by default. Every row matching a path applies (union); no first-match rule.
        - _stage0c gates._ They are not independent of `src/`: `bootstrap/stage0-c/scripts/test_functional.sh` compiles the top-level `tests/functional` and `tests/bootstrap` against the top-level stdlib with `-I src`, and `test_docs` (the `stage0c-docs` gate) compiles `doc/spec/surface_syntax.md` and `doc/spec/intermediate_language.md`. Keep `stage0c-functional` in the `src/`, `stdlib/` and those `tests/` rows, and `stage0c-docs` in the `doc/spec/` row. Read what each other `stage0c-*` target compiles before excluding it.
        - _`stdlib/` is the compiler._ `src/` imports `fsys`, `file`, `os/linux`, `os/unix`, `vec`, `io` and others (`build/zoril -I stdlib=stdlib -I build/gen --emit-deps src/main.zrl`), and zilc and zbuild are built against the stdlib: a `stdlib/` change selects the `src/` tier.

A test file selects itself and `test-strength`; a `*_lib.zrl` or an include root selects the tests that import it (`--emit-deps`), or its whole group if that is not known; a fixture directory selects the scenario that reads it, named in the row.
        - _Scripts._ No generic `scripts/<name>.sh` pattern: each script maps to its gate or self-test by an explicit row, and `scripts/lib/*`, `scripts/test.sh`, `scripts/lib/test_runner.sh` and the `Makefile` select everything.
        - _Canary row._ Derive the files `mutation-self-test` follows from `scripts/mutation_selftest.list` (its second field), not a copy, or test that the row and the list agree.
        - _Docs._ `doc/man/*.scd` selects the `man-pages` scenario; `doc/spec/` selects `stage0c-docs`.
        - _Release path._ Z-0106 says `make dist` and `make dist-check` run every gate; they run no tests (`Makefile`, the `dist` and `dist-check` targets). The run that selects everything is `make preflight`; write that.

Z-0106's savings figures were estimated, not measured: measure the doc tier and the `src/` tier on 16 CPUs and pinned to 4, and record both.

- [x] +testinfra +tests +AI-G Z-0129 T2d `make test-changed` and `preflight-changed`'s skip, wired to the tier table
    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:aab7dc2
    > **Created:** 2026-11-07, split from Z-0115 by the review of Z-0106. **Depends:** Z-0115. **Before:** Z-0121, Z-0112.

    - **Do:** `make test-changed BASE=<commit>` builds the changed-path list in the Makefile only (the series rule: no git in `scripts/test.sh` or the selector): `git diff --name-only --no-renames <base>` (so a rename lists its old path too, and deletions are listed), plus untracked files (`git ls-files --others --exclude-standard`), which `git diff` does not list. It feeds the list to Z-0115's selector on stdin and runs `scripts/test.sh` with what the selector prints. Outside a git checkout (a source tarball), `test-changed` says so and runs everything.
    - **The preflight skip.** `preflight.sh --changed` skips only when every changed path is in a doc row; not, as Z-0106 wrote, when none is flagged `compiled`. Under Z-0106's rule a change to `scripts/preflight.sh`, `scripts/check_reject_diff.sh`, `scripts/mutation-check.sh` or `scripts/test_checked.sh` (flagged `script`) would skip the preflight that runs it. A test-only change still runs the `--checked` pass (`scripts/test_checked.sh`), which may fail where `make test` passes. The skip prints `[SKIP] preflight-changed: ...` naming the base and why, and `make preflight` never skips.

A scenario for `test-changed`: an untracked new test is run; a renamed test's old and new paths are both in the list; outside a checkout everything runs. Take out the untracked-file part and watch its case fail. `make test`, `make preflight-changed`, each `Total:` line quoted.

    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:f485fd3

Documentation outside `doc/tasks/` (`doc/spec/*.md`, `doc/*.md`, `README.md`, `doc/man/*.scd`) cannot be excluded because `make test` gates (`doc-snippets`, `readme-snippets`, `stage0c-docs`, `man-pages`) compile and run code snippets and verify manual pages from them; changes to them can break tests. `doc/tasks/*.tm.md` files contain purely Taskmark metadata and session notes that do not affect the compiler, runtime, standard library, or test suite.

':!doc/tasks'`, removing `doc/tasks build`) capturing working-tree and untracked changes outside `doc/tasks/` and `build/`.         - When all match, `scripts/full_test_stamp.sh check` prints: `make test: tree, compiler and toolchain fingerprint unchanged since earlier run (git:<short>, <date>); stopping (FORCE=1 to run)` and exits 0. On mismatch, prints reason containing `changed since` to stderr and exits 1.

- [x] +testinfra +tests +AI-G Z-0141 T2e `make test-changed` runs everything the tier table selects
    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:9a564d9
    > **Created:** 2026-11-07 by the review of Z-0115 and Z-0129. **Depends:** Z-0129. **Before:** Z-0112.

    - **Why:** the selector picks the right scenarios, but they never reach `scripts/test.sh`, and the tests of Z-0115 and Z-0129 check what is selected rather than what runs. Findings, each reproduced on git:ceb3e40:
        - _Scenarios are dropped._ `scripts/test.sh` has no option that names a scenario, and the selector's default (`--print-args`) mode, which the Makefile uses, prints only `--gate`, `--group` and test paths. `echo scripts/full_test_stamp.sh | sh scripts/select_test_tier.sh` prints `--gate no-new-deps --gate portability`; `--scenarios` on the same input prints `full-test-stamp`. Likewise `scripts/check_md_links.sh` and `doc/spec/*` lose `md-links`, `doc/man/*.scd` loses `man-pages`, and every fixture-directory and self-test row loses its scenario. Z-0115's doc-tier measurement (109 items) is of this short selection. The `--run` mode's workaround (an unknown scenario runs the whole `integration` group) goes.
        - _The stage0c race._ `test-changed` lacks `make test`'s `TEST_GATE_DEPS` (`stage0c-build`, the `zoril-c.release` binary, `$(RT_HOST)`). After `touch bootstrap/stage0-c/src/arena.c`, the selected `scripts/test.sh --quiet --gate portability --gate stage0c-corpus ... --gate stage0c-unit` ended `Total: 233 run, 229 passed, 4 failed, 1 skipped (23.2s)`: `stage0c-corpus`, `stage0c-docs`, `stage0c-error-locations` and `stage0c-unit` failed with `mv: cannot stat 'build/zoril-c.tmp': No such file or directory` (`build/gates/stage0c-unit.log`), the race Z-0109 records.
        - _Test paths dropped when a group is selected._ The selector's `elif`: `printf 'tests/helpers/x.zrl\ntests/functional/assign_type_checked.zrl\n' | sh scripts/select_test_tier.sh` prints `--gate stage0c-functional --gate test-strength --group stdlib`, so the functional test does not run.
        - _A `*_lib.zrl` selects no test._ `echo tests/functional/basic/foo_lib.zrl | sh scripts/select_test_tier.sh` prints only `--gate stage0c-functional --gate test-strength`. Z-0115's correction asked for the tests that import it (`--emit-deps`), or its whole group.
        - _`doc/spec/*` misses `doc-snippets`._ `scripts/test_doc_snippets.sh` compiles `doc/spec/*.md` (its default file list), and the `doc/spec/*` row does not select it.
        - _Tests that cannot see their defect._ `scripts/test_test_changed.sh` cases 2 and 4 run a copy of the git pipeline written in the test, not the Makefile's: taking `--no-renames` out of the Makefile passes. Cases 1 and 3 run the real `make test-changed`, which rebuilds `build/zoril` and the other prerequisites from inside a scenario when they are stale (an AGENTS.md stop condition).
        - _Smaller._ The selector keeps its own copy of the gate list (`ALL_GATES`); it agrees with `scripts/test.sh --list-gates` today, but nothing tests that, so read it from `--list-gates` or test the match. `scripts/test_tier_select.sh:127` uses `\s` in `grep -E` (not POSIX). The selector rereads the table for every path: 300 paths take 2.2 s, so stop at the first path that selects everything (or move the matching into one awk program). An empty change list runs everything; say so in the output.
    - **Do:** a repeatable `--scenario NAME` in `scripts/test.sh` (runs that scenario as its group would, counted and reported the same way), and the selector printing it; union test paths with groups instead of the `elif`, leaving out a path whose group is already selected; a `*_lib.zrl` or include root selects its group; `doc/spec/*` selects `doc-snippets`; `test-changed` depends on `TEST_GATE_DEPS` as `make test` does. The scenario tests drive the real Makefile recipe without rebuilding: pass prerequisites already built, or give the recipe a variable the test can point at a stub, and assert on what the stub runner receives.
    - **Checks:** for each row with a scenario, the selection reaches the runner (a stub `TEST_RUNNER` sees `--scenario NAME`); the stage0c edit above runs all eight gates and passes; the helper-and-functional case runs both; take `--no-renames` and then `git ls-files` out of the Makefile and watch a case fail each time. Remeasure the doc tier on 16 CPUs and pinned to 4 and record both. `make test`, `make preflight-changed`, each `Total:` line quoted.
    - **Implementation and verification (2026-11-07):**
        - Added repeatable `--scenario NAME` option to `scripts/test.sh`, mapping each scenario to its group suite and counting it in suite reporting. Plan generator unions groups, explicit test paths, and standalone scenarios without duplication.
        - Updated `scripts/select_test_tier.sh`: emits `--scenario <name>` in `args` mode (omitting scenarios whose group is selected); removed `--run` mode workaround; unioned `SEL_PATHS` with `SEL_GROUPS` (leaving out paths whose group is selected); mapped `*_lib.zrl` and include roots to their group; updated `ALL_GATES` from `scripts/test.sh --list-gates`; broke early when `ALL_RUN=1`; printed notice to stderr on empty change list.
        - Updated `scripts/test_tiers.tsv`: row `doc/spec/*` now selects both `md-links` and `doc-snippets`.
        - Updated `Makefile`: `TEST_CHANGED_DEPS ?= $(TEST_GATE_DEPS) $(TARGET) $(ZILC) build/zbuild` and `test-changed: $(TEST_CHANGED_DEPS)`, resolving parallel stage0c build race.
        - Updated `scripts/test_test_changed.sh`: executes real `make test-changed TEST_CHANGED_DEPS= TEST_RUNNER="$tmp/stub_runner.sh"` without building binaries; negative tests verify taking `--no-renames` and `git ls-files` out of Makefile causes failures.
        - Fixed POSIX regex `\s` -> `[[:space:]]` in `scripts/test_tier_select.sh` and added test cases verifying `ALL_GATES` match, `--scenario` emission for all scenario rows, helper+functional union, `foo_lib.zrl` group selection, `doc/spec/*` doc-snippets selection, and empty input notice.
        - Verified 8 stage0c gates run cleanly without `zoril-c.tmp` race: `Total: 424 run, 424 passed, 0 failed, 2 skipped (23.4s)`.
        - Measured full doc tier (`doc/spec/surface_syntax.md` with gates + `doc-snippets` + `md-links`): 16 CPUs `Total: 133 run, 133 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (15.4s)`; pinned to 4 CPUs (`taskset -c 0-3`) `Total: 133 run, 133 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (14.3s)`.
        - Verified `make test`: `Total: 3417 run, 3417 passed, 0 failed, 88 skipped (255.8s)`.
        - Verified `make preflight-changed`: `Total: 1265 run, 1265 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (53.5s)`.

- [x] +testinfra +tests +AI-G Z-0142 T2f The unchanged-tree stamp records the tree a run started on
    > **Completed:** 2026-11-07 git:9a564d9
    > **Created:** 2026-11-07 by the review of Z-0121. **Depends:** Z-0121. **Before:** Z-0112.

    - **Why:** `scripts/test.sh` calls `full_test_stamp.sh write` after its last item, and `tree_hash` hashes the tree as it is then. A file edited during the run (about four minutes) is recorded as passed, and the next `make test` stops without testing it. Before Z-0121 this misled only the export guard; now it skips real runs.
    - **Other findings:**
        - `full-test-stamp` case 9 tests a hand-written stub Makefile, not the real `test:` recipe: its `T`, `TSUITE` and `FORCE` conditions are untested, and taking the stamp check out of the real Makefile fails nothing.
        - On a stop, `make test` prints no `Total:` line, so `make test 2>&1 | grep -E '\[FAIL\]|^Total:'` (AGENTS.md) prints nothing. Store the passing run's `Total:` line in the stamp and print it after the stop line.
        - Tools installed after a run do not invalidate it: a run that skipped items for want of lua, rustc or scdoc stays the passing run. Add what `scripts/check_deps.sh` finds (present or absent, and versions where it reads them) to the toolchain fingerprint.
        - Each check runs `git add -A` into a temporary index, which writes a loose object into `.git/objects` for every modified or untracked file. Hash with `git hash-object` (no `-w`) over the changed files, or record why the objects are acceptable.
    - **Do:** `full_test_stamp.sh snapshot` writes the key to `$TR_WORK/stamp.pending` before the first item; `write` moves it to `build/full-test.stamp` only after a whole run that passed. Fix the findings above.
    - **Checks:** a scenario that edits a test file between the snapshot and the write, and a later check that reruns; the real Makefile `test:` recipe, run with a stub runner, stops on an unchanged tree, reruns with `FORCE=1`, with `T=...` and with `TSUITE=language`; the stop prints the earlier `Total:` line; a tool going from absent to present reruns. Take the snapshot out (hash at write time) and watch the first case fail. `make test`, each `Total:` line quoted.
    - **Implementation and verification (2026-11-07):**
        - Added `snapshot` command to `scripts/full_test_stamp.sh` and wired `scripts/test.sh` to record snapshot to `$TR_WORK/stamp.pending` before the first item starts.
        - Updated `write` to atomically move the pending snapshot to `build/full-test.stamp` only after a full passing run, appending `total:<Total: line>`.
        - Updated `check` in `scripts/full_test_stamp.sh` to print the earlier passing run's `Total:` line after the stop message, satisfying `make test 2>&1 | grep -E '\[FAIL\]|^Total:'`.
        - Replaced temporary git index `git add -A` with `git rev-parse HEAD^{tree}` and `git status --porcelain -uall -- . ':!doc/tasks' ':!build'`, hashing changed files with `git hash-object` (no `-w`), writing zero loose objects into `.git/objects`.
        - Added `--fingerprint` to `scripts/check_deps.sh` and included it in `toolchain_fp` so tool presence/absence or version changes invalidate the stamp.
        - Updated `Makefile` `test:` target with `TEST_DEPS ?= $(if $(T),,$(TEST_GATE_DEPS)) $(TARGET) $(ZILC) build/zbuild` and `$(TEST_RUNNER)` invocation.
        - Updated scenario `full-test-stamp` in `scripts/test_full_test_stamp.sh`: verified zero loose objects in `.git/objects`; verified file edited between snapshot and write reruns (and taking snapshot out passes); verified real Makefile recipe with stub runner stops on unchanged tree, prints `Total:` line, reruns on `FORCE=1`, `T=...`, `TSUITE=language`, and fails if stamp check is removed from Makefile; verified tool presence/absence invalidates stamp.
        - Verified `make test`: `Total: 3417 run, 3417 passed, 0 failed, 88 skipped (255.8s)`.
        - Verified `make preflight-changed`: `Total: 1265 run, 1265 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (53.5s)`.

With unpushed commits, the documented doc-tier rerun after a task-file commit covers all of them unless `BASE=HEAD~1` is passed.

Decide for each: replace, keep with the reason recorded, or move into one opt-in group that skips cleanly without git or outside a checkout. Record the failures git has caused (from the task files and the report) to rank them. Implementation follows as its own task.
    - **Rule set by the maintainer (2026-11-07):** `make test` uses no git (`AGENTS.md`, Portability), so "keep" is no longer an answer for anything `make test` reaches. Each use it reaches is either replaced (a file list by `find` over the source directories; a key or cache by file contents) or moved to `make preflight`, which requires git (a self-test of a script whose job is git: `test-changed`, `build-info`, `preflight-selftest`, `reject-diff-selftest`, the git parts of `stray-files-selftest`, `fuzz-tools`, `fuzz-record` and `fuzz-fresh`, and the `clean-tree` gate). Uses reached only from `make preflight-changed` and later may stay, with their reason. Also say which git uses `make test-changed` and `make preflight-changed` need, and whether each could take its list of changed paths some other way. The list is the input of Z-0144; known today: `check_md_tables.sh` takes its file list from `git ls-files` with no `find` fallback, so without git it checks nothing and passes (reproduced: a 154-character table planted in `doc/`, git off `PATH`: `line 166: git: command not found`, then `[PASS] md-tables: no table is wider than 140 characters`, rc 0; with git it fails on that file). The tarball run counted it as a pass.
    - **Order (2026-11-07):** moved up from after Z-0140 to follow Z-0143.

"Reached" means reached from `make test`. Decision per use, under the rule (`AGENTS.md`, Portability):
        - _A. File lists, reached; replace with one `find` helper (Z-0144)._ `scripts/check_no_new_deps.sh:82` and `scripts/check_md_escapes.sh:48` (both already fall back to `find`); `scripts/check_md_tables.sh:166` (no fallback: checks nothing without git and passes, reproduced on Z-0111 above); `scripts/check_fuzz_fresh.sh:116` (the Markdown files that quote the fuzz record).

        - _C. Self-tests of tools whose job is git, reached; move to a group `make preflight` runs (Z-0144)._ Scenarios `test-changed`, `build-info` (`scripts/test_build_info.sh`), `preflight-selftest` (`scripts/test_preflight.sh`: `preflight_base.sh`, `selfhost-check.sh`, the differential), `reject-diff-selftest` (`scripts/test_reject_diff.sh`: the differential's `git worktree`); the git cases of `fuzz-tools` (scratch repositories for `record_update.sh` and the fuzz preflight); the `git archive` case of `check-deps-selftest` (`scripts/test_check_deps.sh:280`); the `.gitignore` case of `qbe-fingerprint` (`scripts/test_qbe_fingerprint.sh:43`, a repository-hygiene check). Gates: `clean-tree` (`scripts/check_clean_tree.sh`: a clean tree is a git notion), and `fuzz-record`'s `--release` refusal cases (`scripts/check_fuzz_record.sh:151`, a release check).

        - _E. Labels and lookups with a fallback, reached; replace or stop reaching them._ `scripts/env.sh:20` (repository root, the last of three lookups; the `dirname` lookup above it suffices: drop it). `scripts/mutation-check.sh:74` (`--changed` with no base runs `git diff HEAD`; the `mutation-self-test` gate reaches it through its malformed-list case, `scripts/test_mutation_check.sh:90`: give that case its changed list another way). `scripts/bench_cross_lang.sh:287` (a commit label: `--check-contention`, which `bench-contention` uses, exits at line 265 before it, but `--check-thresholds-only`, which `bench-threshold-gate` uses, runs on to line 599 and reaches it; read the build's recorded commit instead). `bootstrap/stage0-c/Makefile:24` (`GIT_HASH := $(shell git rev-parse ...)`, evaluated by every `stage0c-*` gate's `make -C`; falls back to `unknown`). `scripts/version.sh:11` (`git describe` when `.git` exists, else `VERSION`), reached by the `tool-versions` scenario (`scripts/test_tool_versions.sh:9`; `test_build_info.sh` copies it, and moves under C).

Not reached from `make test`; contributor or maintainer role, kept._ `Makefile` `test-changed` (its changed-path list), `scripts/preflight.sh`, `scripts/lib/preflight_base.sh`, `scripts/mutation-check.sh` with a base, `scripts/check_reject_diff.sh`, `scripts/selfhost-check.sh`, `scripts/repro-check.sh`, `scripts/dist.sh`, `scripts/dist_user_paths.sh`, `scripts/fuzz/preflight.sh`, `scripts/fuzz/record_update.sh`, `scripts/record_marker_density.sh`, `scripts/generate_build_info.sh` (the build's commit, with a fallback; `make`, not `make test`), and stage0-c's `lint` and `test_infra` targets (`bootstrap/stage0-c/scripts/verify_test_failure.sh:8`), which no gate runs.
        - _`test-changed` without git._ Its list needs a base; Z-0144's content listing offers one that git does not: the files whose hash differs from the last passing whole run's listing ("changed since the last passing `make test`"). Recorded as an option for after Z-0144, not part of it.
        - _Failures git has caused, from the task files and this series:_ signing and hook settings in user config failing scratch commits (Z-0081); a self-test assuming `HEAD~1` exists (fixed in git:ceb3e40); the stamp keyed on `HEAD^{tree}`, so every commit and every `tm sweep` reruns the suite (Z-0143's findings, open until Z-0144); a tarball failing `make test` with exit 127 (fixed by Z-0143, reported correctly by Z-0148); `check_md_tables.sh` passing without checking (above); `stray-files` skipping in a tarball, so a tarball user's run never sees stray files.
    - **Awaiting review:** two decisions are the maintainer's. (1) `bootstrap/stage0-c/Makefile:24` is not a script, so the freeze exception in `AGENTS.md` does not cover it; recommended: drop `GIT_HASH` (it labels stage0-c's own output only), or have the top `Makefile` pass it in. (2) `scripts/version.sh` in tests: recommended: tests read the version the build recorded (`build/gen`) instead of calling `version.sh`, and `version.sh` keeps `git describe` for `make` in a checkout, with `VERSION` as the fallback a tarball uses. Everything else above is decided by the rule and goes to Z-0144.
    - **Decided (the maintainer, 2026-11-07):** both recommendations, for Z-0144 to carry out:
        - _`GIT_HASH`._ It is compiled into `zoril-c --version` (`bootstrap/stage0-c/Makefile:110` writes it into a generated header; `src/zoril-c.c:37` prints it), and `bootstrap/stage0-c/tests/Makefile:33` repeats the `$(shell git ...)`. Replace both with `GIT_HASH ?= unknown`, so no stage0-c `make` runs git; the top `Makefile` may pass the hash when it builds stage0-c. First check that a gate's `make -C` with the default does not regenerate the header and rebuild a binary the build made with a passed value (that would bring back the `build/zoril-c.tmp` race); if it would, pass nothing. Neither change touches the seed compiler's code.
        - _The version in tests._ `scripts/test_tool_versions.sh:9` takes the expected version from `build/gen/version.zrl`, which the build wrote, instead of running `scripts/version.sh`; `version.sh` keeps `git describe` for `make` in a checkout and `VERSION` in a tarball. The test exists to catch a binary whose version stamp does not match the tree: if reading the build's own record leaves that check with nothing to catch under `make test` (whose prerequisites rebuild the binaries), say so and move the stale-stamp case to `make preflight` rather than keep a check that cannot fail.
    - **Completion:** the inventory is in git:0186bf5 and the maintainer's two decisions are above; no code changed. Checks: `tm validate doc/tasks/*.tm.md`, `scripts/check_md_fmt.sh`, and the doc tier (`scripts/test.sh --quiet --gate comment-noise --gate md-check --gate safety-model --scenario md-links`): `Total: 110 run, 110 passed, 0 failed, 0 skipped (15.3s)`.
